14. using System;
15. using System.Net;
16. using System.Net.Http;
17. using System.Threading.Tasks;
18. 
19. namespace SDKTemplate.Helpers
20. {
21.     public class CommonLicenseRequest
22.     {
23.         private string lastErrorMessage;
24.         private HttpClient httpClient;
25.         public string GetLastErrorMessage()
26.         {
27.             return lastErrorMessage;
28.         }
29.         public CommonLicenseRequest(HttpClient client = null)
30.         {
31.             if (client == null)
32.                 httpClient = new HttpClient();
33.             else
34.                 httpClient = client;
35.             lastErrorMessage = string.Empty;
36.         }
37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// Invoked to acquire the PlayReady license.
39.         /// </summary>
40.         /// <param name="licenseServerUri">License Server URI to retrieve the PlayReady license.</param>
41.         /// <param name="httpRequestContent">HttpContent including the Challenge transmitted to the PlayReady server.</param>
42.         public async virtual Task<HttpContent> AcquireLicenseAsync(Uri licenseServerUri, HttpContent httpRequestContent)
43.         {
44.             try
45.             {
46.                 httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("msprdrm_server_redirect_compat", "false");
47.                 httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("msprdrm_server_exception_compat", "false");
48. 
49.                 HttpResponseMessage response = await httpClient.PostAsync(licenseServerUri, httpRequestContent);
50.                 response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();
51. 
52.                 if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.OK)
53.                 {
54.                     lastErrorMessage = string.Empty;
55.                     return response.Content;
56.                 }
57.                 else
58.                 {
59.                     lastErrorMessage = "AcquireLicense - Http Response Status Code: " + response.StatusCode.ToString();
60.                 }
61.             }
62.             catch (Exception exception)
63.             {
64.                 lastErrorMessage = exception.Message;
65.                 return null;
66.             }
67.             return null;
68.         }
69.     }

269.             }
270.             else

273.                 m_PlaybackPositionMinTime = TimeSpan.Zero;
274.             }

277.             var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
278.             {
279.                 StartPosition.Text = m_PlaybackPositionMinTime.ToString("c");
280.                 EndPosition.Text = m_PlaybackPositionMaxTime.ToString("c");
281.             });
282. 
283.             string ranges = "";
284.             foreach (var range in SeekableRanges)
285.             {

287.             }
288.             ranges = ranges.TrimEnd(',');
289.             Log($"MediaPlayer_PlaybackSession_SeekableRangesChanged: {ranges}");
290.         }

313. 
314.         /// <summary>
315.         /// We will use slider ticks to drive a playback Position.
316.         ///
317.         /// This is a linear interpolation, so:
318.         /// y = m*x + b
319.         /// Where:
320.         /// Position = m*sliderPosition + b
321.         ///
322.         /// Where we have some data points:
323.         ///
324.         /// At MinPosition, we are at slider position=0
325.         /// MinPosition = m * 0 + b
326.         /// Thus:
327.         ///   b = MinPosition
328.         ///
329.         /// We choose a 100000 tick granularity for the slider,
330.         /// at which point it will be at MaxPosition:
331.         /// MaxPosition = m * 100000 + b
332.         /// MaxPosition = m * 100000 + MinPosition
333.         ///
334.         /// Thus:
335.         ///   m = (MaxPosition - MinPosition) / 100000
336.         ///   m = (MaxPosition - MinPosition) / sliderFullScale
337.         ///
338.         /// And more generally, we have:
339.         ///
340.         /// PlaybackPosition =  [(MaxPosition - MinPosition) / sliderFullScale] * SliderPosition + MinPosition
341.         /// or:
342.         /// PlaybackPosition =  ((MaxPosition - MinPosition) * SliderPosition / sliderFullScale)   + MinPosition
343.         ///
344.         /// </summary>
345.         public TimeSpan GetPlaybackPosition(double sliderPosition, double sliderFullScale, TimeSpan MinPosition, TimeSpan MaxPosition)
346.         {
347.             if (sliderFullScale == 0)
348.                 return TimeSpan.Zero;
349.             return TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)((MaxPosition - MinPosition).Ticks * sliderPosition / sliderFullScale) + MinPosition.Ticks);
350.         }

72.         private void Page_OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
73.         {

76.             // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the MediaPlayer:
77.             mediaPlayerLogger = new MediaPlayerLogger(LoggerControl, mediaPlayer);
78. 
79.             // In addition to logging, we use the callbacks to update some UI elements in this scenario:
80.             RegisterForDiscreteControls(mediaPlayer);
81.             RegisterForPositionUpdateControls(mediaPlayer);
82.

86.             mediaPlayerElement.SetMediaPlayer(mediaPlayer);

91.                 null,
92.                 LoggerControl,
93.                 LoadSourceFromUriAsync);
94.

96.             ContentSelectorControl.SetAutoPlay(true);
97.             mediaPlayerElement.AreTransportControlsEnabled = false;
98.             LoggerControl.Height = 200;

102. 
103.         private async Task<MediaPlaybackItem> LoadSourceFromUriAsync(Uri uri, HttpClient httpClient = null)
104.         {
105.             UnregisterHandlers(mediaPlayerElement.MediaPlayer);
106. 
107.             AdaptiveMediaSourceCreationResult result = null;
108.             if (httpClient != null)
109.             {
110.                 result = await AdaptiveMediaSource.CreateFromUriAsync(uri, httpClient);
111.             }
112.             else
113.             {
114.                 result = await AdaptiveMediaSource.CreateFromUriAsync(uri);
115.             }
116. 
117.             MediaSource source;
118.             if (result.Status == AdaptiveMediaSourceCreationStatus.Success)
119.             {
120.                 var adaptiveMediaSource = result.MediaSource;
121. 
122.                 // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the AdaptiveMediaSource:
123.                 adaptiveMediaSourceLogger = new AdaptiveMediaSourceLogger(LoggerControl, adaptiveMediaSource);
124. 
125.                 source = MediaSource.CreateFromAdaptiveMediaSource(adaptiveMediaSource);
126. 
127.                 if (adaptiveMediaSource.MaxSeekableWindowSize.HasValue)
128.                 {
129.                     UpdateSeekableWindowControls(adaptiveMediaSource);
130.                 }
131.             }
132.             else
133.             {
134.                 Log($"Error creating the AdaptiveMediaSource. Status: {result.Status}, ExtendedError.Message: {result.ExtendedError.Message}, ExtendedError.HResult: {result.ExtendedError.HResult.ToString("X8")}");
135.                 return null;
136.             }
137. 
138.             // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the MediaSource:
139.             mediaSourceLogger = new MediaSourceLogger(LoggerControl, source);
140. 
141.             // Save the original Uri.
142.             source.CustomProperties["uri"] = uri.ToString();
143. 
144.             // You're likely to put a content tracking id into the CustomProperties.
145.             source.CustomProperties["contentId"] = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
146. 
147.             var mpItem = new MediaPlaybackItem(source);
148. 
149.             // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the MediaPlaybackItem:
150.             mediaPlaybackItemLogger = new MediaPlaybackItemLogger(LoggerControl, mpItem);
--- RESULT ---
1. ﻿using System;
2. using System.Collections.Generic;
3. using System.Linq;
4. using System.Text;
5. using System.Threading.Tasks;
6. using Windows.Media.Core;
7. 
8. namespace SDKTemplate
9. {
10.     /// <summary>
11.     /// The custom data that can be created, scheduled then consumed as part of a TimedMetadataTrack which has been added by the app.
12.     /// 
13.     /// In these samples, it creates a set of cues at the start, 1/4, 1/2, 3/4 and upon completion of playback.  
14.     /// For each of these points, it will fire an MediaCue with the custom TrackingEventUri property to be consumed by
15.     /// the app in the CueEntered event handler of the TimedMetadataTrack.
16.     /// 
17.     /// We also demonstrate the use of the .Duration IMediaCue property, which will fire CueExited for the midpoint Cue at the 55% mark.
18.     /// </summary>
19.     public class TrackingEventCue : IMediaCue
20.     {
21.         public TimeSpan Duration { get; set; }
22.         public string Id { get; set; }
23.         public TimeSpan StartTime { get; set; }
24.         public Uri TrackingEventUri { get; set; }
25. 
26.         /// <summary>
27.         /// Creates a TimedMetadataTrack on the source
28.         /// </summary>
29.         /// <param name="source">the source on which to create a TimedMetadataTrack</param>
30.         /// <param name="contentId">the content id for this source</param>
31.         /// <param name="reportingUriFormat">the format string used to create reporting Uri for each event</param>
32.         public static void CreateTrackingEventsTrack(MediaSource source, string contentId, string reportingUriFormat)
33.         {
34.             if (source.Duration.HasValue && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(contentId))
35.             {
36.                 long sourceDurationTicks = source.Duration.Value.Ticks;
37.                 sourceDurationTicks = AdjustDurationIfLive(sourceDurationTicks);
38. 
39.                 var trackingEvents = new TimedMetadataTrack("TrackingEvents", "", TimedMetadataKind.Custom);
40.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
41.                 {
42.                     // This method is called once the media has loaded. If it is set to AutoPlay, then
43.                     // we may miss Position=0.0. Avoid the race by "starting" at the 2-second mark.
44.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2),
45.                     Id = "2s",
46.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "started", contentId)),
47.                 });
48.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
49.                 {
50.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.25)),
51.                     Id = "0.25",
52.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "firstQuartile", contentId)),
53.                 });
54.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
55.                 {
56.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.50)),
57.                     Id = "0.50",
58.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "midpoint", contentId)),
59.                     // We can cause CueExited to fire at 55% by setting a Duration:
60.                     Duration = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.05)),
61.                 });
62.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
63.                 {
64.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.75)),
65.                     Id = "0.75",
66.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "thirdQuartile", contentId)),
67.                 });
68.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
69.                 {
70.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks(sourceDurationTicks),
71.                     Id = "1.00",
72.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "complete", contentId)),
73.                 });
74.                 source.ExternalTimedMetadataTracks.Add(trackingEvents);
75.             }
76.         }
77. 
78.         public static long AdjustDurationIfLive(long sourceDurationTicks)
79.         {
80.             // Live sources will have an duration of TimeSpan.MaxValue,
81.             // so assume they are 2 minutes long for these scenarios.
82.             if (sourceDurationTicks == TimeSpan.MaxValue.Ticks)
83.             {
84.                 sourceDurationTicks = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2).Ticks;
85.             }
86.             return sourceDurationTicks;
87.         }
88.     }
89. }

250.                 adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadRequested -= DownloadRequested;

254. 
255.         private async void DownloadRequested(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadRequestedEventArgs args)
256.         {
257.             if (args.ResourceType == AdaptiveMediaSourceResourceType.Key)
258.             {
259.                 switch (tokenMethod)
260.                 {
261.                     case AzureKeyAcquisitionMethod.None:
262.                         break;
263.                     case AzureKeyAcquisitionMethod.AuthorizationHeader:
264.                         // By updating the IHttpFilter KeyHost property here, we ensure it's up to date
265.                         // before the network request is made.  It is the IHttpFilter that will insert
266.                         // the Authorization header.
267.                         if (httpClientFilter != null)
268.                         {
269.                             httpClientFilter.KeyHost = args.ResourceUri.Host;
270.                         }
271.                         break;
272.                     case AzureKeyAcquisitionMethod.UrlQueryParameter:
273.                         ModifyKeyRequestUri(args);
274.                         break;
275.                     case AzureKeyAcquisitionMethod.ApplicationDownloaded:
276.                         await AppDownloadedKeyRequest(args);
277.                         break;
278.                     default:
279.                         break;
280.                 }
281.             }
282.         }
283. 
284.         private void ModifyKeyRequestUri(AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadRequestedEventArgs args)
285.         {
286.             if (ContentSelectorControl.SelectedModel == null)
287.             {
288.                 return;
289.             }
290. 
291.             // This pattern can be used to modify Uris, for example:
292.             //   To a redirect traffic to a secondary endpoint
293.             //   To change an segment request into a byte-range Uri into another resource
294. 
295.             // Add the Bearer token to the Uri and modify the args.Result.ResourceUri
296.             string armoredAuthToken = System.Net.WebUtility.UrlEncode("Bearer=" + ContentSelectorControl.SelectedModel.AesToken);
297.             string uriWithTokenParameter = $"{args.ResourceUri.AbsoluteUri}&token={armoredAuthToken}";
298.             args.Result.ResourceUri = new Uri(uriWithTokenParameter);
299.         }
300. 
301.         private async Task AppDownloadedKeyRequest(AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadRequestedEventArgs args)
302.         {
303.             if (ContentSelectorControl.SelectedModel == null)
304.             {
305.                 return;
306.             }
307. 
308.             // For AzureKeyAcquisitionMethod.ApplicationDownloaded we do the following:
309.             //   Call .GetDeferral() to allow asynchronous work to be performed
310.             //   Get the requested network resource using app code
311.             //   Set .Result.InputStream or .Result.Buffer with the response data
312.             //   Complete the deferral to indicate that we are done.
313.             // With this pattern, the app has complete control over any downloaded content.
314. 
315.             // Obtain a deferral so we can perform asynchronous operations.
316.             var deferral = args.GetDeferral();
317.             try
318.             {
319.                 var appHttpClientForKeys = new HttpClient();
320.                 appHttpClientForKeys.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new HttpCredentialsHeaderValue("Bearer", ContentSelectorControl.SelectedModel.AesToken);
321. 
322.                 HttpRequestMessage request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Get, args.ResourceUri);
323. 
324.                 HttpResponseMessage response = await appHttpClientForKeys.SendRequestAsync(
325.                     request, HttpCompletionOption.ResponseHeadersRead).AsTask(ctsForAppHttpClientForKeys.Token);
326. 
327.                 if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
328.                 {
329.                     args.Result.InputStream = await response.Content.ReadAsInputStreamAsync();
330.                     // Alternatively, we could use:
331.                     // args.Result.Buffer = await response.Content.ReadAsBufferAsync();
332.                 }
333.                 else
334.                 {
335.                     // The app code failed. Report this by setting the args.Result.ExtendedStatus.
336.                     // This will ensure that the AdaptiveMediaSource does not attempt to download the resource
337.                     // itself, and instead treats the download as having failed.
338.                     switch (response.StatusCode)
339.                     {
340.                         case HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized:
341.                             // HTTP_E_STATUS_DENIED
342.                             args.Result.ExtendedStatus = 0x80190191;
343.                             break;
344.                         case HttpStatusCode.NotFound:
345.                             // HTTP_E_STATUS_NOT_FOUND
346.                             args.Result.ExtendedStatus = 0x80190194;
347.                             break;
348.                         default:
349.                             // HTTP_E_STATUS_UNEXPECTED
350.                             args.Result.ExtendedStatus = 0x80190001;
351.                             break;
352.                     }
353.                     Log($"Key Download Failed: {response.StatusCode} {args.ResourceUri}");
354.                 }
355.             }
356.             catch (TaskCanceledException)
357.             {
358.                 Log($"Request canceled: {args.ResourceUri}");
359. 
360.             }
361.             catch (Exception e)
362.             {
363.                 Log($"Key Download Failed: {e.Message} {args.ResourceUri}");
364.             }
365.             finally
366.             {
367.                 // Complete the deferral when done.
368.                 deferral.Complete();
369.             }
370.         }
371. 
372.         private void DownloadFailed(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadFailedEventArgs args)
373.         {
374.             Log($"DownloadFailed: {args.HttpResponseMessage}, {args.ResourceType}, {args.ResourceUri}");
375.         }

84.         /// <summary>
85.         /// Changes the logging level at which messages are filtered.
86.         /// </summary>

88.         /// <param name="e"></param>

94.         /// <summary>
95.         /// Provides logging with filtering based on UI settings.
96.         /// Logging functions tangential to main scenarios should specify a log view level less than Always.
97.         /// </summary>
98.         /// <param name="message">message to be logged</param>
99.         /// <param name="level">message category</param>
100.         public async void Log(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level = LogViewLoggingLevel.Always)
101.         {
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 LogFromUIThread(message, level);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
109.                 {
110.                     LogFromUIThread(message, level);
111.                 });
112.             }
113.         }
114. 
115.         private void LogFromUIThread(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level)
116.         {
117.             string logEntry = $"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff} - {message}";
118. 
119.             if (LoggingLevel <= level)
120.             {
121.                 SolidColorBrush foregroundBrush = LoggingBrushes[(int)level];
122. 
123.                 if (IsLogViewFull)
124.                 {
125.                     // Once the view is full, messages go only to the debug console.
126.                     System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);
127.                     return;
128.                 }
129. 
130.                 if (LoggingListBox.Items.Count >= MaxItemsInLogView)
131.                 {
132.                     IsLogViewFull = true;
133. 
134.                     System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);
135.                     logEntry = "Log window is full";
136.                     foregroundBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
137.                 }
138. 
139.                 LoggingListBox.Items.Insert(0, new TextBlock() { Text = logEntry, Foreground = foregroundBrush });
140.             }
141.             else if (level != LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose)
142.             {
143.                 // Non-Verbose messages blocked from the LogView are shown on the debug console.
144.                 System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"{level}: {logEntry}");
145.             }
146.         }

152.             Clipboard.SetContent(content);

--- RESULT ---
350.         }
351. 
352.         /// <summary>
353.         /// Given:
354.         /// y = m * x + b
355.         /// x = (y - b) /m
356.         ///
357.         /// Where from above:
358.         ///  b = MinPosition
359.         ///  m = (MaxPosition - MinPosition) / sliderFullScale
360.         ///
361.         /// We have:
362.         ///  Position = (PlaybackPosition - MinPosition) * sliderFullScale / (MaxPosition - MinPosition)
363.         ///
364.         /// </summary>
365.         public double GetSliderPositionAtPlaybackPosition(TimeSpan playbackPosition, double sliderFullScale, TimeSpan MinPosition, TimeSpan MaxPosition)
366.         {
367.             if ((MaxPosition - MinPosition).Ticks == 0)
368.                 return 0;
369. 
370.             if (playbackPosition == TimeSpan.Zero)
371.             {
372.                 LoggerControl.Log("Transient playbackPosition == TimeSpan.Zero", LogViewLoggingLevel.Error);
373.             }
374. 
375.             var interpolatedPosition = (playbackPosition - MinPosition).Ticks * sliderFullScale / (MaxPosition - MinPosition).Ticks;
376. 
377.             if (interpolatedPosition < 0)
378.             {
379.                 interpolatedPosition = 0;
380.                 LoggerControl.Log("Transient calculatedPosition < 0", LogViewLoggingLevel.Error);
381.             }
382. 
383.             return interpolatedPosition;
384.         }

255.         private void RegisterMetadataHandlers(MediaPlaybackItem mediaPlaybackItem, int index)
256.         {
257.             var timedTrack = mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks[index];
258.             object customValue = null;
259.             mediaPlaybackItem.Source.CustomProperties.TryGetValue("contentId", out customValue);
260.             string contentId = (string)customValue;
261. 
262.             StringBuilder logMsg = new StringBuilder($"{contentId} ");
263. 
264.             if (timedTrack.TrackKind == MediaTrackKind.TimedMetadata &&
265.                 (timedTrack.TimedMetadataKind == TimedMetadataKind.Data ||
266.                 timedTrack.TimedMetadataKind == TimedMetadataKind.Custom))
267.             {
268.                 // Check for known dispatchTypes:
269.                 var dispatchType = mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks[index].DispatchType;
270.                 if (String.Equals(dispatchType, "EXTM3U", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
271.                 {
272.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_extm3u_CueEntered;
273.                     timedTrack.Label = "HLS Manifest comments";
274.                     // Tell the platform that the app will render these.
275.                     // Without this, the CueEntered event will not be raised by the platform.
276.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
277.                 }
278.                 else if (string.Equals(dispatchType, "15260DFFFF49443320FF49443320000F", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) // per Apple TS spec for ID3 meta
279.                 {
280.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_id3_CueEntered;
281.                     timedTrack.Label = "ID3 Tags";
282.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
283.                 }
284.                 else if (string.Equals(dispatchType, "emsg:mp4", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) // For 'emsg' box embedded in mp4 container
285.                 {
286.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_emsg_mp4_CueEntered;
287.                     timedTrack.Label = "emsg payload";
288.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
289.                 }
290. 
291.                 // Check for our Custom TimedMetadataTrack
292.                 if (mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks[index].Id == "TrackingEvents")
293.                 {
294.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_TrackingEvents_CueEntered;
295.                     timedTrack.CueExited += metadata_TrackingEvents_CueExited;
296.                     timedTrack.Label = "Tracking Events";
297.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
298.                 }
299. 
300.                 logMsg.AppendLine($"Registered CueEntered for {timedTrack.Label}.");
301.             }
302.             else
303.             {
304.                 logMsg.AppendLine("Did not register CueEntered for TimedMetadataTrack.");
305.             }
306.             logMsg.AppendLine($"TimedMetadataKind: {timedTrack.TimedMetadataKind}, Id: {timedTrack.Id}, Label: {timedTrack.Label}, DispatchType: {timedTrack.DispatchType}, Language: {timedTrack.Language}");
307. 
308.             Log(logMsg.ToString());
309.         }
310. 
311.         private void UnregisterMetadataHandlers(TimedMetadataTrack timedTrack)
312.         {
313.             if (timedTrack.Label == "HLS Manifest comments")
314.             {
315.                 timedTrack.CueEntered -= metadata_extm3u_CueEntered;
316.             }
317.             if(timedTrack.Label == "ID3 Tags")
318.             {
319.                 timedTrack.CueEntered -= metadata_id3_CueEntered;
320.             }
321.             if(timedTrack.Label == "emsg payload")
322.             {
323.                 timedTrack.CueEntered -= metadata_emsg_mp4_CueEntered;
324.             }
325.             if (timedTrack.Label == "Tracking Events")
326.             {
327.                 timedTrack.CueEntered -= metadata_TrackingEvents_CueEntered;
328.                 timedTrack.CueExited -= metadata_TrackingEvents_CueExited;
329.             }
330.         }

332. 
333.         private void metadata_extm3u_CueEntered(TimedMetadataTrack timedMetadataTrack, MediaCueEventArgs args)
334.         {
335.             StringBuilder logMsg = new StringBuilder();
336. 
337.             object customValue = null;
338.             timedMetadataTrack.PlaybackItem.Source.CustomProperties.TryGetValue("contentId", out customValue);
339.             string contentId = (string)customValue;
340.             logMsg.AppendLine($"{contentId} M3U8 DataCue CueEntered raised.");
341.             var dataCue = args.Cue as DataCue;
342.             if (dataCue != null && dataCue.Data != null)
343.             {
344.                 // The payload is a UTF-16 Little Endian null-terminated string.
345.                 // It is any comment line in a manifest that is not part of the HLS spec.
346.                 var dr = DataReader.FromBuffer(dataCue.Data);
347.                 dr.UnicodeEncoding = Windows.Storage.Streams.UnicodeEncoding.Utf16LE;
348.                 var m3u8Comment = dr.ReadString(dataCue.Data.Length / 2 - 1);
349.                 logMsg.AppendLine($"Label: {timedMetadataTrack.Label}, Id: {dataCue.Id}, StartTime: {dataCue.StartTime}, Duration: {dataCue.Duration}");
350.                 logMsg.Append(m3u8Comment);
351.                 // TODO: Use the m3u8Comment string for something useful.
352.             }
353.             Log(logMsg.ToString());
354.         }
355. 
356.         private void metadata_id3_CueEntered(TimedMetadataTrack timedMetadataTrack, MediaCueEventArgs args)
357.         {
358.             StringBuilder logMsg = new StringBuilder();
359.             object customValue = null;
360.             timedMetadataTrack.PlaybackItem.Source.CustomProperties.TryGetValue("contentId", out customValue);
361.             string contentId = (string)customValue;
362.             logMsg.AppendLine($"{contentId} ID3 DataCue CueEntered raised.");
363.             var dataCue = args.Cue as DataCue;
364.             if (dataCue != null && dataCue.Data != null && dataCue.Data.Length >= 10)
365.             {
366.                 // The payload is the raw ID3 bytes found in a TS stream
367.                 // Ref: http://id3.org/id3v2.4.0-structure
368.                 var dr = DataReader.FromBuffer(dataCue.Data);
369.                 var header_ID3 = dr.ReadString(3);
370.                 var header_version_major = dr.ReadByte();
371.                 var header_version_minor = dr.ReadByte();
372.                 var header_flags = dr.ReadByte();
373.                 var header_tagSize = dr.ReadUInt32();
374.                 // TODO: Use the ID3 bytes for something useful.
375. 
376.                 logMsg.AppendLine($"Label: {timedMetadataTrack.Label}, Id: {dataCue.Id}, StartTime: {dataCue.StartTime}, Duration: {dataCue.Duration}");
377.                 logMsg.Append($"{header_ID3}, {header_version_major}.{header_version_minor}, {header_flags}, {header_tagSize}");
378.             }
379.             Log(logMsg.ToString());
380.         }
381.

393.                 // Ref: ISO/IEC 20009-1, Section 5.10.3.3.2 Definition of Box Type: 'emsg'

395.                 string value = string.Empty;

415.                 timescale = propValue != null ? (UInt32)propValue : timescale;

418.                 propValue = null;
419.                 dataCue.Properties.TryGetValue(presentation_time_delta_key, out propValue);
420.                 presentation_time_delta = propValue != null ? (UInt32)propValue : presentation_time_delta;

with:\n```csharp\n//*********************************************************\n//\n// Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.\n// This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).\n// THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF\n// ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY\n// IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR\n// PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.\n//\n//*********************************************************\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer;\nusing Windows.UI;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media;\n\nnamespace AdaptiveStreamingApp.Logging\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Logging levels supported by the LogView control.\n    /// The \"Always\" level is supported only by the Log method.\n    /// </summary>\n    public enum LogViewLoggingLevel\n    {\n        Verbose = 0,\n        Information,\n        Warning,\n        Error,\n        Critical,\n        Always,\n    }\n\n    /// \n    /// <summary>\n    /// This control can be used to log events from a scenario page.\n    /// \n    /// In the XAML markup, you can set the initial logging level by setting\n    /// LoggingLevel=\"Verbose\", or any other enum value of LogViewLoggingLevel.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed partial class LogView : UserControl\n    {\n        public LogView()\n        {\n            this.InitializeComponent();\n\n            LoggingBrushes = new SolidColorBrush[] {\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"VerboseLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"InformationLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"WarningLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"ErrorLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"CriticalLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"AlwaysLogBrush\"],\n            };\n\n            LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)LoggingLevel;\n        }\n\n        public static readonly DependencyProperty LogLevelProperty =\n            DependencyProperty.Register(\"LoggingLevel\", typeof(LogViewLoggingLevel), typeof(LogView),\n                new PropertyMetadata(LogViewLoggingLevel.Warning, OnLoggingLevelChanged));\n\n        public LogViewLoggingLevel LoggingLevel\n        {\n            get { return (LogViewLoggingLevel)GetValue(LogLevelProperty); }\n            set { SetValue(LogLevelProperty, value); }\n        }\n\n        private static void OnLoggingLevelChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            var logger = (LogView)d;\n            var loggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)e.NewValue;\n            logger.LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)loggingLevel;\n        }\n\n        bool IsLogViewFull = false;\n        int MaxItemsInLogView = 500;\n\n        SolidColorBrush[] LoggingBrushes;\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Changes the logging level at which messages are filtered.\n        /// </summary>\n        private void LogLevel_Changed(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            LoggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Provides logging with filtering based on UI settings.\n        /// </summary>\n        public void Log(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level = LogViewLoggingLevel.Always)\n        {\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                LogFromUIThread(message, level);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() =>\n                {\n                    LogFromUIThread(message, level);\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void LogFromUIThread(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level)\n        {\n            string logEntry = $\"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff} - {message}\";\n\n            if (LoggingLevel <= level)\n            {\n                SolidColorBrush foregroundBrush = LoggingBrushes[(int)level];\n\n                if (IsLogViewFull)\n                {\n                    // Once the view is full, messages go only to the debug console.\n                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (LoggingListBox.Items.Count >= MaxItemsInLogView)\n                {\n                    IsLogViewFull = true;\n\n                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);\n                    logEntry = \"Log window is full\";\n                    foregroundBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);\n                }\n\n                LoggingListBox.Items.Insert(0, new TextBlock() { Text = logEntry, Foreground = foregroundBrush });\n            }\n            else if (level != LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose)\n            {\n                // Non-Verbose messages blocked from the LogView are shown on the debug console.\n                System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($\"{level}: {logEntry}\");\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void CopyLogToClipboard_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            var content = new DataPackage();\n            content.SetText(String.Join(\"\\r\\n\", LoggingListBox.Items.Select(item => (item as TextBlock).Text)));\n            Clipboard.SetContent(content);\n        }\n\n        private void ClearLog_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            ClearLog();\n        }\n\n        public void ClearLog()\n        {\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                LoggingListBox.Items.Clear();\n                IsLogViewFull = false;\n            }\n            else\n AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadBitrateChangedEventArgs args)\n        {\n            uint downloadBitrate = args.NewValue;\n            UpdateDownloadBitrateAsync(downloadBitrate);\n        }\n\n        private void UpdateDownloadBitrateAsync(uint downloadBitrate)\n        {\n            DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() =>\n            {\n                iconDownloadBitrate.Symbol = bitrateHelper.GetBitrateSymbol(downloadBitrate);\n                txtDownloadBitrate.Text = downloadBitrate.ToString();\n            });\n        }\n\n        private void PlaybackBitrateChanged(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourcePlaybackBitrateChangedEventArgs args)\n        {\n            uint playbackBitrate = args.NewValue;\n            UpdatePlaybackBitrateAsync(playbackBitrate);\n        }\n\n        private void UpdatePlaybackBitrateAsync(uint playbackBitrate)\n        {\n            DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() =>\n            {\n                iconPlaybackBitrate.Symbol = bitrateHelper.GetBitrateSymbol(playbackBitrate);\n                txtPlaybackBitrate.Text = playbackBitrate.ToString();\n            });\n        }\n        #endregion\n\n\n        #region Utilities\n        private void Log(string message)\n        {\n            LoggerControl.Log(message);\n        }\n        MediaPlayerLogger mediaPlayerLogger;\n x:Class=\"AdaptiveStreamingApp.Logging.LogView\"\n    xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\"\n    xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\"\n    xmlns:d=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/expression/blend/2008\"\n    xmlns:mc=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/markup-compatibility/2006\"\n    mc:Ignorable=\"d\"\n    d:DesignHeight=\"300\"\n    d:DesignWidth=\"400\">\n\n    <UserControl.Resources>\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"VerboseLogBrush\" Color=\"Gray\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"InformationLogBrush\" Color=\"White\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"WarningLogBrush\" Color=\"Yellow\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"ErrorLogBrush\" Color=\"Red\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"CriticalLogBrush\" Color=\"Magenta\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"AlwaysLogBrush\" Color=\"YellowGreen\" />\n    </UserControl.Resources>\n\n    <StackPanel>\n        <StackPanel Orientation=\"Horizontal\">\n            <Button Click=\"CopyLogToClipboard_Click\" Content=\"Copy log\" Margin=\"0,0,10,0\"/>\n            <Button Click=\"ClearLog_Click\" Content=\"Clear log\" Margin=\"0,0,10,0\"/>\n            <TextBlock Text=\"Logging level:\" VerticalAlignment=\"Center\" Margin=\"0,0,10,0\"/>\n            <ComboBox x:Name=\"LogLevelComboBox\" SelectionChanged=\"LogLevel_Changed\">\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Verbose\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Information\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Warning\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Error\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Critical\"/>\n            </ComboBox>\n        </StackPanel>\n        <ScrollViewer x:Name=\"ListBoxScrollViewer\" Margin=\"0,10,0,0\" Height=\"100\" HorizontalScrollMode=\"Enabled\" HorizontalScrollBarVisibility=\"Visible\" VerticalScrollMode=\"Enabled\" VerticalScrollBarVisibility=\"Visible\">\n            <ListBox x:Name=\"LoggingListBox\">\n                <ListBox.ItemContainerStyle>\n                    <Style TargetType=\"ListBoxItem\">\n                        <Setter Property=\"Padding\" Value=\"0\"/>\n                    </Style>\n                </ListBox.ItemContainerStyle>\n            </ListBox>\n        </ScrollViewer>\n    </StackPanel>\n</UserControl>\n```\n\n### LogView.xaml.cs\n\nCreate `Logging\\LogView.xaml.cs` with:\n```csharp\n//*********************************************************\n//\n// Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.\n// This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).\n// THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF\n// ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY\n// IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR\n// PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.\n//\n//*********************************************************\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer;\nusing Windows.UI;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media;\n\nnamespace AdaptiveStreamingApp.Logging\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Logging levels supported by the LogView control.\n    /// The \"Always\" level is supported only by the Log method.\n    /// </summary>\n    public enum LogViewLoggingLevel\n    {\n        Verbose = 0,\n        Information,\n        Warning,\n        Error,\n        Critical,\n        Always,\n    }\n\n    /// \n    /// <summary>\n    /// This control can be used to log events from a scenario page.\n    /// \n    /// In the XAML markup, you can set the initial logging level by setting\n    /// LoggingLevel=\"Verbose\", or any other enum value of LogViewLoggingLevel.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed partial class LogView : UserControl\n    {\n        public LogView()\n        {\n            this.InitializeComponent();\n\n            LoggingBrushes = new SolidColorBrush[] {\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"VerboseLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"InformationLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"WarningLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"ErrorLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"CriticalLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"AlwaysLogBrush\"],\n            };\n\n            LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)LoggingLevel;\n        }\n\n        public static readonly DependencyProperty LogLevelProperty =\n            DependencyProperty.Register(\"LoggingLevel\", typeof(LogViewLoggingLevel), typeof(LogView),\n                new PropertyMetadata(LogViewLoggingLevel.Warning, OnLoggingLevelChanged));\n\n        public LogViewLoggingLevel LoggingLevel\n        {\n            get { return (LogViewLoggingLevel)GetValue(LogLevelProperty); }\n            set { SetValue(LogLevelProperty, value); }\n        }\n\n        private static void OnLoggingLevelChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            var logger = (LogView)d;\n            var loggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)e.NewValue;\n            logger.LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)loggingLevel;\n        }\n\n        bool IsLogViewFull = false;\n        int MaxItemsInLogView = 500;\n\n        SolidColorBrush[] LoggingBrushes;\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Changes the logging level at which messages are filtered.\n        /// </summary>\n        private void LogLevel_Changed(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            LoggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Provides logging with filtering based on UI settings.\n        /// </summary>\n        public void Log(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level = LogViewLoggingLevel.Always)\n        {\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                LogFromUIThread(message, level);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() =>\n                {\n                    LogFromUIThread(message, level);\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void LogFromUIThread(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level)\n        {\n            string logEntry = $\"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff} - {message}\";\n\n            if (LoggingLevel <= level)\n            {\n                SolidColorBrush foregroundBrush = LoggingBrushes[(int)level];\n\n                if (IsLogViewFull)\n                {\n                    // Once the view is full, messages go only to the debug console.\n                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (LoggingListBox.Items.Count >= MaxItemsInLogView)\n                {\n                    IsLogViewFull = true;\n\n                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);\n                    logEntry = \"Log window is full\";\n                    foregroundBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);\n                }\n\n                LoggingListBox.Items.Insert(0, new TextBlock() { Text = logEntry, Foreground = foregroundBrush });\n            }\n            else if (level != LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose)\n            {\n                // Non-Verbose messages blocked from the LogView are shown on the debug console.\n                System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($\"{level}: {logEntry}\");\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void CopyLogToClipboard_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            var content = new DataPackage();\n            content.SetText(String.Join(\"\\r\\n\", LoggingListBox.Items.Select(item => (item as TextBlock).Text)));\n            Clipboard.SetContent(content);\n        }\n\n        private void ClearLog_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            ClearLog();\n        }\n\n        public void ClearLog()\n        {\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                LoggingListBox.Items.Clear();\n                IsLogViewFull = false;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(ClearLog);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Logging source files\n\nFor each of these files, read them from the UWP source directory 24.         public const string FEATURE_NAME = "AdaptiveStreaming";
25. 
26.         List<Scenario> scenarios = new List<Scenario>
27.         {
28.             new Scenario() { Title="Simplest Adaptive Streaming", ClassType=typeof(Scenario1_SimplestAdaptiveStreaming)},
29.             new Scenario() { Title="Event Handlers", ClassType=typeof(Scenario2_EventHandlers)},
30.             new Scenario() { Title="Network Request Modification", ClassType=typeof(Scenario3_RequestModification)},
31.             new Scenario() { Title="Adaptive Streaming Tuning", ClassType=typeof(Scenario4_Tuning)},
32.             new Scenario() { Title="Metadata", ClassType=typeof(Scenario5_Metadata)},
33.             new Scenario() { Title="Ad Insertion", ClassType=typeof(Scenario6_AdInsertion)},
34.             new Scenario() { Title="Live Seekable Range", ClassType=typeof(Scenario7_LiveSeekableRange)},
35.         };
36. 
37.         public static IReadOnlyList<AdaptiveContentModel> ContentManagementSystemStub = AdaptiveContentModel.GetKnownAzureMediaServicesModels();
38.         public static AdaptiveContentModel FindContentById(int id)
39.         {
40.             return ContentManagementSystemStub.First(model => model.Id == id);
41.         }
42.     }
43. 
44.     public class Scenario
45.     {
46.         public string Title { get; set; }
47.         public Type ClassType { get; set; }
48.     }
49. 
50.     public class BitrateHelper
51.     {
52.         // Each bitrate is assigned a symbol as follows:
53.         // The bottom quartile gets 1 bar, the second quartile gets 2 bars,
54.         // the third quartile gets 3 bars, and the top quartile gets 4 bars.
55.         private Dictionary<uint, Symbol> symbolAssignment = new Dictionary<uint, Symbol>();
56. 
57.         public BitrateHelper(IEnumerable<uint> availableBitRates)
58.         {
59.             uint[] sortedBitRates = availableBitRates.ToArray();
60.             Array.Sort(sortedBitRates);
61.             int i = 0;
62.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 1 / 4)
63.             {
64.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.OneBar);
65.             }
66.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 2 / 4)
67.             {
68.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.TwoBars);
69.             }
70.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 3 / 4)
71.             {
72.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.ThreeBars);
73.             }
74.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 4 / 4)
75.             {
76.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.FourBars);
77.             }
78.         }
79. 
80.         public Symbol GetBitrateSymbol(uint currentBitrate)
81.         {
82.             Symbol symbol = Symbol.ZeroBars;
83.             if (symbolAssignment.TryGetValue(currentBitrate, out symbol))
84.             {
85.                 return symbol;
86.             }
87.             return Symbol.ZeroBars;
88.         }
89.     }
90. }

x:Class=\"AdaptiveStreamingApp.Logging.LogView\"\n    xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\"\n    xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\"\n    xmlns:d=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/expression/blend/2008\"\n    xmlns:mc=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/markup-compatibility/2006\"\n    mc:Ignorable=\"d\"\n    d:DesignHeight=\"300\"\n    d:DesignWidth=\"400\">\n\n    <UserControl.Resources>\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"VerboseLogBrush\" Color=\"Gray\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"InformationLogBrush\" Color=\"White\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"WarningLogBrush\" Color=\"Yellow\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"ErrorLogBrush\" Color=\"Red\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"CriticalLogBrush\" Color=\"Magenta\" />\n        <SolidColorBrush x:Key=\"AlwaysLogBrush\" Color=\"YellowGreen\" />\n    </UserControl.Resources>\n\n    <StackPanel>\n        <StackPanel Orientation=\"Horizontal\">\n            <Button Click=\"CopyLogToClipboard_Click\" Content=\"Copy log\" Margin=\"0,0,10,0\"/>\n            <Button Click=\"ClearLog_Click\" Content=\"Clear log\" Margin=\"0,0,10,0\"/>\n            <TextBlock Text=\"Logging level:\" VerticalAlignment=\"Center\" Margin=\"0,0,10,0\"/>\n            <ComboBox x:Name=\"LogLevelComboBox\" SelectionChanged=\"LogLevel_Changed\">\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Verbose\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Information\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Warning\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Error\"/>\n                <ComboBoxItem Content=\"Critical\"/>\n            </ComboBox>\n        </StackPanel>\n        <ScrollViewer x:Name=\"ListBoxScrollViewer\" Margin=\"0,10,0,0\" Height=\"100\" HorizontalScrollMode=\"Enabled\" HorizontalScrollBarVisibility=\"Visible\" VerticalScrollMode=\"Enabled\" VerticalScrollBarVisibility=\"Visible\">\n            <ListBox x:Name=\"LoggingListBox\">\n                <ListBox.ItemContainerStyle>\n                    <Style TargetType=\"ListBoxItem\">\n                        <Setter Property=\"Padding\" Value=\"0\"/>\n                    </Style>\n                </ListBox.ItemContainerStyle>\n            </ListBox>\n        </ScrollViewer>\n    </StackPanel>\n</UserControl>\n```\n\n### LogView.xaml.cs\nCreate Logging\\LogView.xaml.cs with:\n```csharp\n//*********************************************************\n//\n// Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.\n// This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).\n// THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF\n// ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY\n// IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR\n// PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.\n//\n//*********************************************************\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer;\nusing Windows.UI;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media;\n\nnamespace AdaptiveStreamingApp.Logging\n{\n    public enum LogViewLoggingLevel\n    {\n        Verbose = 0,\n        Information,\n        Warning,\n        Error,\n        Critical,\n        Always,\n    }\n\n    public sealed partial class LogView : UserControl\n    {\n        public LogView()\n        {\n            this.InitializeComponent();\n\n            LoggingBrushes = new SolidColorBrush[] {\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"VerboseLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"InformationLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"WarningLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"ErrorLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"CriticalLogBrush\"],\n                (SolidColorBrush)Resources[\"AlwaysLogBrush\"],\n            };\n\n            LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)LoggingLevel;\n        }\n\n        public static readonly DependencyProperty LogLevelProperty =\n            DependencyProperty.Register(\"LoggingLevel\", typeof(LogViewLoggingLevel), typeof(LogView),\n                new PropertyMetadata(LogViewLoggingLevel.Warning, OnLoggingLevelChanged));\n\n        public LogViewLoggingLevel LoggingLevel\n        {\n            get { return (LogViewLoggingLevel)GetValue(LogLevelProperty); }\n            set { SetValue(LogLevelProperty, value); }\n        }\n\n        private static void OnLoggingLevelChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            var logger = (LogView)d;\n            var loggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)e.NewValue;\n            logger.LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)loggingLevel;\n        }\n\n        bool IsLogViewFull = false;\n        int MaxItemsInLogView = 500;\n\n        SolidColorBrush[] LoggingBrushes;\n\n        private void LogLevel_Changed(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            LoggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex;\n        }\n\n        public void Log(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level = LogViewLoggingLevel.Always)\n        {\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                LogFromUIThread(message, level);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() =>\n                {\n                    LogFromUIThread(message, level);\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void LogFromUIThread(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level)\n        {\n            string logEntry = $\"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff} - {message}\";\n\n            if (LoggingLevel <= level)\n            {\n                SolidColorBrush foregroundBrush = LoggingBrushes[(int)level];\n\n                if (IsLogViewFull)\n                {\n                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                if (LoggingListBox.Items.Count >= MaxItemsInLogView)\n                {\n                    IsLogViewFull = true;\n\n                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);\n                    logEntry = \"Log window is full\";\n                    foregroundBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);\n                }\n\n                LoggingListBox.Items.Insert(0, new TextBlock() { Text = logEntry, Foreground = foregroundBrush });\n            }\n            else if (level != LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose)\n            {\n                System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($\"{level}: {logEntry}\");\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void CopyLogToClipboard_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            var content = new DataPackage();\n            content.SetText(String.Join(\"\\r\\n\", LoggingListBox.Items.Select(item => (item as TextBlock).Text)));\n            Clipboard.SetContent(content);\n        }\n\n        private void ClearLog_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            ClearLog();\n        }\n\n        public void ClearLog()\n        {\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                LoggingListBox.Items.Clear();\n                IsLogViewFull = false;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(ClearLog);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Logging source files\nRead each file from UWP source 43. 
44.     public class Scenario
45.     {
46.         public string Title { get; set; }
47.         public Type ClassType { get; set; }
48.     }
49. 
50.     public class BitrateHelper
51.     {
52.         // Each bitrate is assigned a symbol as follows:
53.         // The bottom quartile gets 1 bar, the second quartile gets 2 bars,
54.         // the third quartile gets 3 bars, and the top quartile gets 4 bars.
55.         private Dictionary<uint, Symbol> symbolAssignment = new Dictionary<uint, Symbol>();
56. 
57.         public BitrateHelper(IEnumerable<uint> availableBitRates)
58.         {
59.             uint[] sortedBitRates = availableBitRates.ToArray();
60.             Array.Sort(sortedBitRates);
61.             int i = 0;
62.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 1 / 4)
63.             {
64.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.OneBar);
65.             }
66.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 2 / 4)
67.             {
68.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.TwoBars);
69.             }
70.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 3 / 4)
71.             {
72.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.ThreeBars);
73.             }
74.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 4 / 4)
75.             {
76.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.FourBars);
77.             }
78.         }
79. 
80.         public Symbol GetBitrateSymbol(uint currentBitrate)
81.         {
82.             Symbol symbol = Symbol.ZeroBars;
83.             if (symbolAssignment.TryGetValue(currentBitrate, out symbol))
84.             {
85.                 return symbol;
86.             }
87.             return Symbol.ZeroBars;
88.         }
89.     }
90. }

14. using System;
15. using System.Net;
16. using System.Net.Http;
17. using System.Threading.Tasks;
18. 
19. namespace SDKTemplate.Helpers
20. {
21.     public class CommonLicenseRequest
22.     {
23.         private string lastErrorMessage;
24.         private HttpClient httpClient;
25.         public string GetLastErrorMessage()
26.         {
27.             return lastErrorMessage;
28.         }
29.         public CommonLicenseRequest(HttpClient client = null)
30.         {
31.             if (client == null)
32.                 httpClient = new HttpClient();
33.             else
34.                 httpClient = client;
35.             lastErrorMessage = string.Empty;
36.         }
37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// Invoked to acquire the PlayReady license.
39.         /// </summary>
40.         /// <param name="licenseServerUri">License Server URI to retrieve the PlayReady license.</param>
41.         /// <param name="httpRequestContent">HttpContent including the Challenge transmitted to the PlayReady server.</param>
42.         public async virtual Task<HttpContent> AcquireLicenseAsync(Uri licenseServerUri, HttpContent httpRequestContent)
43.         {
44.             try
45.             {
46.                 httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("msprdrm_server_redirect_compat", "false");
47.                 httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("msprdrm_server_exception_compat", "false");
48. 
49.                 HttpResponseMessage response = await httpClient.PostAsync(licenseServerUri, httpRequestContent);
50.                 response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();
51. 
52.                 if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.OK)
53.                 {
54.                     lastErrorMessage = string.Empty;
55.                     return response.Content;
56.                 }
57.                 else
58.                 {
59.                     lastErrorMessage = "AcquireLicense - Http Response Status Code: " + response.StatusCode.ToString();
60.                 }
61.             }
62.             catch (Exception exception)
63.             {
64.                 lastErrorMessage = exception.Message;
65.                 return null;
66.             }
67.             return null;
68.         }
69.     }
70. }

1. ﻿using System;
2. using System.Collections.Generic;
3. using System.Linq;
4. using System.Text;
5. using System.Threading.Tasks;
6. using Windows.Media.Core;
7. 
8. namespace SDKTemplate
9. {
10.     /// <summary>
11.     /// The custom data that can be created, scheduled then consumed as part of a TimedMetadataTrack which has been added by the app.
12.     /// 
13.     /// In these samples, it creates a set of cues at the start, 1/4, 1/2, 3/4 and upon completion of playback.  
14.     /// For each of these points, it will fire an MediaCue with the custom TrackingEventUri property to be consumed by
15.     /// the app in the CueEntered event handler of the TimedMetadataTrack.
16.     /// 
17.     /// We also demonstrate the use of the .Duration IMediaCue property, which will fire CueExited for the midpoint Cue at the 55% mark.
18.     /// </summary>
19.     public class TrackingEventCue : IMediaCue
20.     {
21.         public TimeSpan Duration { get; set; }
22.         public string Id { get; set; }
23.         public TimeSpan StartTime { get; set; }
24.         public Uri TrackingEventUri { get; set; }
25. 
26.         /// <summary>
27.         /// Creates a TimedMetadataTrack on the source
28.         /// </summary>
29.         /// <param name="source">the source on which to create a TimedMetadataTrack</param>
30.         /// <param name="contentId">the content id for this source</param>
31.         /// <param name="reportingUriFormat">the format string used to create reporting Uri for each event</param>
32.         public static void CreateTrackingEventsTrack(MediaSource source, string contentId, string reportingUriFormat)
33.         {
34.             if (source.Duration.HasValue && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(contentId))
35.             {
36.                 long sourceDurationTicks = source.Duration.Value.Ticks;
37.                 sourceDurationTicks = AdjustDurationIfLive(sourceDurationTicks);
38. 
39.                 var trackingEvents = new TimedMetadataTrack("TrackingEvents", "", TimedMetadataKind.Custom);
40.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
41.                 {
42.                     // This method is called once the media has loaded. If it is set to AutoPlay, then
43.                     // we may miss Position=0.0. Avoid the race by "starting" at the 2-second mark.
44.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2),
45.                     Id = "2s",
46.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "started", contentId)),
47.                 });
48.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
49.                 {
50.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.25)),
51.                     Id = "0.25",
52.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "firstQuartile", contentId)),
53.                 });
54.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
55.                 {
56.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.50)),
57.                     Id = "0.50",
58.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "midpoint", contentId)),
59.                     // We can cause CueExited to fire at 55% by setting a Duration:
60.                     Duration = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.05)),
61.                 });
62.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
63.                 {
64.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)Math.Round(sourceDurationTicks * 0.75)),
65.                     Id = "0.75",
66.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "thirdQuartile", contentId)),
67.                 });
68.                 trackingEvents.AddCue(new TrackingEventCue()
69.                 {
70.                     StartTime = TimeSpan.FromTicks(sourceDurationTicks),
71.                     Id = "1.00",
72.                     TrackingEventUri = new Uri(string.Format(reportingUriFormat, "complete", contentId)),
73.                 });
74.                 source.ExternalTimedMetadataTracks.Add(trackingEvents);
75.             }
76.         }
77. 
78.         public static long AdjustDurationIfLive(long sourceDurationTicks)
79.         {
80.             // Live sources will have an duration of TimeSpan.MaxValue,
81.             // so assume they are 2 minutes long for these scenarios.
82.             if (sourceDurationTicks == TimeSpan.MaxValue.Ticks)
83.             {
84.                 sourceDurationTicks = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2).Ticks;
85.             }
86.             return sourceDurationTicks;
87.         }
88.     }
89. }
90.

12. using SDKTemplate;
13. using SDKTemplate.Logging;
14. using System;
15. using System.Collections.Generic;
16. using System.Net.Http;
17. using System.Net.Http.Headers;
18. using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
19. using System.Text;
20. using System.Threading.Tasks;
21. using Windows.Foundation.Collections;
22. using Windows.Media.Playback;
23. using Windows.Media.Protection;
24. using Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady;
25. using Windows.UI.Core;
26. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;

28. namespace SDKTemplate.Helpers
29. {
30. 
31.     /// <summary>
32.     /// Encapsulates all the required calls to setup a MediaProtectionManger within a MediaPlayer
33.     /// for typical PlayReady scenarios.
34.     /// 
35.     /// It is left to the user of these samples to explore other PlayReady rights management scenarios.
36.     /// 
37.     /// </summary>
38.     public class PlayReadyHelper
39.     {
40.         public PlayReadyHelper(LogView logger = null)
41.         {
42.             this.logger = logger;
43.         }
44.         private LogView logger;
45. 
46. 
47.         private const int MSPR_E_CONTENT_ENABLING_ACTION_REQUIRED = -2147174251;
48.         private KeyValuePair<string, object> PlayReadyWinRTTrustedInput = new KeyValuePair<string, object>("{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}", "Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady.PlayReadyWinRTTrustedInput" );
49.         private KeyValuePair<string, object> MediaProtectionSystemId = new KeyValuePair<string, object>("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemId", "{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}");
50.         private KeyValuePair<string, object> MediaProtectionContainerGuid = new KeyValuePair<string, object>("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionContainerGuid", "{9A04F079-9840-4286-AB92-E65BE0885F95}");
51.         private const string MediaProtectionSystemIdMapping = "Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemIdMapping";
52. 
53.         // NOTE: For custom scenarios, you may need these:
54.         public string playReadyLicenseUrl = "";
55.         public string playReadyChallengeCustomData = "";
56. 
57. 
58.         private void Log(string message)
59.         {
60.             if (logger != null)
61.             {
62.                 logger.Log(message, LogViewLoggingLevel.Information);
63.             }
64.             else
65.             {
66.                 string logEntry = $"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff} - {message}";
67.                 System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);
68.             }
69.         }
70. 
71.         public void SetUpProtectionManager(MediaPlayer mediaPlayer)
72.         {
73.             Log("Enter SetUpProtectionManager");
74. 
75.             if(mediaPlayer == null)
76.                 throw new ArgumentException("SetUpProtectionManager was passed a null MediaPlayer");
77.             
78.             Log("Creating protection system mappings...");
79.             var protectionManager = new MediaProtectionManager();
80. 
81.             protectionManager.ComponentLoadFailed += new ComponentLoadFailedEventHandler(ProtectionManager_ComponentLoadFailed);
82.             protectionManager.ServiceRequested += new ServiceRequestedEventHandler(ProtectionManager_ServiceRequested);
83. 
84.             // The code here is mandatory and should be just copied directly over to the app
85.             // Ref: https://msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/windows/desktop/aa376846%28v=vs.85%29.aspx
86. 
87.             // Setup PlayReady as the ProtectionSystem to use by mediaFoundation: 
88.             var contentProtectionSystems = new PropertySet();
89.             contentProtectionSystems.Add(PlayReadyWinRTTrustedInput);
90.             protectionManager.Properties.Add(MediaProtectionSystemIdMapping, contentProtectionSystems);
91.             protectionManager.Properties.Add(MediaProtectionSystemId);
92.             protectionManager.Properties.Add(MediaProtectionContainerGuid);
93. 
94.             mediaPlayer.ProtectionManager = protectionManager;
95. 
96.             Log("Leave SetUpProtectionManager");
97.         }
98. 
99.         private async void ProtectionManager_ServiceRequested(MediaProtectionManager sender, ServiceRequestedEventArgs e)
100.         {
101.             Log("Enter ProtectionManager_ServiceRequested");
102. 
103.             if (e.Request is PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest)
104.             {
105.                 var individualizationRequest = e.Request as PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest;
106.                 bool bResultIndiv = await ReactiveIndividualizationRequestAsync(individualizationRequest, e.Completion);
107.             }
108.             else if (e.Request is PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest)
109.             {
110.                 var licenseRequest = e.Request as PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest;
111.                 LicenseAcquisitionRequest(licenseRequest, e.Completion, playReadyLicenseUrl, playReadyChallengeCustomData);
112.             }
113. 
114.             Log("Leave ProtectionManager_ServiceRequested");
115.         }
116. 
117.         private void ProtectionManager_ComponentLoadFailed(MediaProtectionManager sender, ComponentLoadFailedEventArgs e)
118.         {
119.             Log("Enter ProtectionManager_ComponentLoadFailed");
120.             Log(e.Information.ToString());
121. 
122.             // List the failing components - RevocationAndRenewalInformation
123.             foreach (var item in e.Information.Items)
124.             {
125.                 Log(item.Name + 
126.                     "\nReasons=0x" + item.Reasons + 
127.                     "\nRenewal Id=" + item.RenewalId);
128.             }
129.             e.Completion.Complete(false);
130. 
131.             Log("Leave ProtectionManager_ComponentLoadFailed");
132.         }
133. 
134.         /// <summary>
135.         /// Invoked to acquire the PlayReady License
136.         /// </summary>
137.         async void LicenseAcquisitionRequest(PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest licenseRequest, MediaProtectionServiceCompletion completionNotifier, string url, string challengeCustomData)
138.         {
139.             bool bResult = false;
140.             string ExceptionMessage = string.Empty;
141. 
142.             try
143.             {
144.                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
145.                 {
146.                     Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady Manual License Acquisition Service Request in progress - URL: " + url);
147. 
148.                     if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(challengeCustomData))
149.                     {
150.                         var encoding = new UTF8Encoding();
151.                         byte[] b = encoding.GetBytes(challengeCustomData);
152.                         licenseRequest.ChallengeCustomData = Convert.ToBase64String(b, 0, b.Length);
153.                     }
154. 
155.                     PlayReadySoapMessage soapMessage = licenseRequest.GenerateManualEnablingChallenge();
156. 
157.                     byte[] messageBytes = soapMessage.GetMessageBody();
158.                     HttpContent httpContent = new ByteArrayContent(messageBytes);
159. 
160.                     IPropertySet propertySetHeaders = soapMessage.MessageHeaders;
161.                     foreach (string strHeaderName in propertySetHeaders.Keys)
162.                     {
163.                         string strHeaderValue = propertySetHeaders[strHeaderName].ToString();
164. 
165.                         // The Add method throws an ArgumentException try to set protected headers like "Content-Type"
166.                         // so set it via "ContentType" property
167.                         if (strHeaderName.Equals("Content-Type", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
168.                             httpContent.Headers.ContentType = MediaTypeHeaderValue.Parse(strHeaderValue);
169.                         else
170.                             httpContent.Headers.Add(strHeaderName.ToString(), strHeaderValue);
171.                     }
172.                     var licenseAcquision = new CommonLicenseRequest();
173.                     HttpContent responseHttpContent = await licenseAcquision.AcquireLicenseAsync(new Uri(url), httpContent);
174.                     if (responseHttpContent != null)
175.                     {
176.                         Exception exResult = licenseRequest.ProcessManualEnablingResponse(await responseHttpContent.ReadAsByteArrayAsync());
177.                         if (exResult != null)
178.                         {
179.                             throw exResult;
180.                         }
181.                         bResult = true;
182.                     }
183.                     else
184.                         ExceptionMessage = licenseAcquision.GetLastErrorMessage();
185.                 }
186.                 else
187.                 {
188.                     Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady License Acquisition Service Request in progress - URL: " + licenseRequest.Uri.ToString());
189.                     await licenseRequest.BeginServiceRequest();
190.                     bResult = true;
191.                 }
192.             }
193.             catch (Exception e)
194.             {
195.                 ExceptionMessage = e.Message;
196.             }
197. 
198.             if (bResult == true)
199.                 Log(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url) ? "ProtectionManager Manual PlayReady License Acquisition Service Request successful" :
200.                     "ProtectionManager PlayReady License Acquisition Service Request successful");
201.             else
202.                 Log(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url) ? "ProtectionManager Manual PlayReady License Acquisition Service Request failed: " + ExceptionMessage :
203.                     "ProtectionManager PlayReady License Acquisition Service Request failed: " + ExceptionMessage);
204.             completionNotifier.Complete(bResult);
205.         }
206.         /// <summary>
207.         /// Proactive Individualization Request 
208.         /// </summary>
209.         async void ProactiveIndividualizationRequest()
210.         {
211.             var individualizationRequest = new PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest();
212.             Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady ProActive Individualization Service Request in progress...");
213.             bool bResultIndiv = await ReactiveIndividualizationRequestAsync(individualizationRequest, null);
214.             if (bResultIndiv == true)
215.                 Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady ProActive Individualization Service Request successful");
216.             else
217.                 Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady ProActive Individualization Service Request failed");
218. 
219.         }
220.         /// <summary>
221.         /// Invoked to send the Individualization Request 
222.         /// </summary>
223.         async Task<bool> ReactiveIndividualizationRequestAsync(PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest individualizationRequest, MediaProtectionServiceCompletion completionNotifier)
224.         {
225.             bool bResult = false;
226.             Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady Individualization Service Request in progress...");
227.             try
228.             {
229.                 await individualizationRequest.BeginServiceRequest();
230.                 bResult = true;
231.             }
232.             catch (COMException comException) when (comException.HResult == MSPR_E_CONTENT_ENABLING_ACTION_REQUIRED)
233.             {
234.                 individualizationRequest.NextServiceRequest();
235.             }
236.             catch (Exception)
237.             {
238.             }
239. 
240.             if (bResult == true)
241.                 Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady Individualization Service Request successful");
242.             else
243.                 Log("ProtectionManager PlayReady Individualization Service Request failed");
244.             if (completionNotifier != null) completionNotifier.Complete(bResult);
245.             return bResult;
246. 
247.         }
248.         
249.     }
250. }

45.         {

48.

50.

57.                 prHelper.SetUpProtectionManager(mp);
58.                 mediaPlayerElement.SetMediaPlayer(mp);
59.             }
60. 
61.             mediaPlayerElement.Source = MediaSource.CreateFromUri(adaptiveContentModel.ManifestUri);
62.         }

12. using SDKTemplate.Helpers;
13. using SDKTemplate.Logging;
14. using System;
15. using System.Linq;
16. using System.Threading;
17. using System.Threading.Tasks;
18. using Windows.Media.Core;

20. using Windows.Media.Streaming.Adaptive;

29.     /// See the README.md for discussion of this scenario.
